```
def fft_conv(a,b):
   n=len(a); m=len(b) if b else len(a)
   size=1; while size < n+m-1: size*=2
   fa=np.fft.rfft(a,size)
   if b is None: fb=fa.copy()? but if a==b, use fa*fa (not conj)
   else: fb=np.fft.rfft(b,size)
   out=np.fft.irfft(fa*fb,size)[:n+m-1]
```

Single coordinate optimal update: Given a, if we vary a_j while keeping sum fixed by scaling all else? Not optimal. Pair transfer: move mass from i to j, keeping sum fixed. Could incremental update convolution for pair transfer:
When changing a_i by -t, a_j by +t (others fixed). Convolution coefficients change linearly and quadratically. New conv[k] = old[k] + δ_i(k)+δ_j(k)+ t^2 * indicator k=i+j? More precisely conv = sum_l a_l a_{k-l}. If change Δ_i=-δ? Let d_i=-t at i, d_j=+t at j. New conv = old + sum_l a_l d_{k-l} + sum_l d_l a_{k-l} + sum_l d_l d_{k-l}. The cross terms can be computed from a shifted: for each j_change, add t*(a shifted) for each change. For pair transfer, O(n) to update conv if we know affected lags: i and j shifts of a. We can maintain conv array. For score max, need max; updating max exactly O(n) to recompute or maintain heap? O(n) sufficient for n=512; many steps maybe 50M Python? delta = conv.copy()

def surrogate_score(a, mode='full'):
    n=len(a); S=np.sum(a); if S<=1e-300: return inf
    conv=fft_convolve(a)
    if mode=='full': m=np.max(conv)
    elif mode in ('off','off_peak'):
       if n==1: return 0? For full n=1 off no lags. But score? If hidden off maybe 0. set -inf? Return 0.
       idx=np.arange(len(conv)); mask=idx!=(n-1); m=np.max(conv[mask]) if any else 0
    elif mode=='positive':
       start=n; # indices n to 2n-2? For positive lag 1..n-1: indexes n..2n-2
       if start>=len(conv): m=0 else m=np.max(conv[start:])
    else max all.
    return 2.0*n*m/(S*S)
```

def mask_indices(mode,n):
   K=2*n-1
   idx=np.arange(K)
   if mode=='full': return idx
   if mode=='off': return idx[idx != n-1]
   if mode=='positive': return idx[idx >= n] # lag >=1
   if mode=='negative': return idx[idx < n-1]
   return idx[idx != n-1]
```

    # a np array
    out=[]
    for x in a:
       v=float(x)
       if v<0: v=0.0
       elif v>1000: v=1000.0

`smooth_max_grad(a, mode, tau, conv=None)`
```
n=len(a); K=2*n-1; idx=get_mask_indices(mode,n)
if idx.size==0: return 0, zeros, zeros?

# to avoid overflow if tau tiny, vals-maxc <=0 so exp <=1; okay.

if sum_exp <=0: ...
value=maxc + tau*np.log(sum_exp)
w_full=np.zeros(K)
w_full[idx]=exps/sum_exp
size=next_fft_size(K)
fa=np.fft.rfft(a,size)
fw=np.fft.rfft(w_full,size)
# gradient_j = 2 * sum_k w_k a_{k-j}; Use ifft(conj(fw)*fa)
gconv=np.fft.irfft(np.conj(fw)*fa, size)[:n]
grad=2.0*gconv

def pair_update_conv(conv, a, n, i, j, t):
    K=2*n-1
    shift_i=np.zeros(K); shift_j=np.zeros(K)
    end_i=min(K,i+n); if i<end_i: shift_i[i:end_i]=a[:end_i-i]
    end_j=...
    delta = 2.0*t*(shift_j - shift_i)
    if i != j:
       if 2*i < K: delta[2*i] += t*t
       if 2*j < K: delta[2*j] += t*t
       if i+j < K: delta[i+j] -= 2*t*t
    return conv + delta
```

### Detailed function implementations

`next_fft_size(m)`:
```
def next_fft_size(m):
    size=1
    while size < m:
        size <<= 1
    return size
```
Could use `int(2**np.ceil(np.log2(m)))`, but loop fine.

`fft_convolve(a, b=None)`:
```
def fft_convolve(a, b=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(a)
    if b is None:
       b = a
       m = n
    else:
       b = np.asarray(b,dtype=np.float64); m=len(b)
    size=next_fft_size(n+m-1)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,size)
    if b is a: # can't check? if b is None
       fb = fa
       # Need fa*fa, but if we use fb=fa, product fa*fa; if we later modify? no.
    else:
       fb=np.fft.rfft(b,size)
    out=np.fft.irfft(fa*fb,size)
    out[:n+m-1]
```
If b is a view? We'll pass None for auto.

`get_mask_indices(mode,n)`:
```
def get_mask_indices(mode,n):
    key=(mode,n)
    cached = _MASK_CACHE.get(key)
    if cached is not None: return cached
    K=2*n-1
    idx=np.arange(K, dtype=np.int64)
    if mode=='full' or mode=='all':
        m=idx
    elif mode in ('off','off_peak','offcenter'):
        if n==1:
            m=np.array([], dtype=np.int64)
        else:
            mask=idx != (n-1)
            m=idx[mask]
    elif mode=='positive':
        start=n
        if start>=K:
           m=np.array([], dtype=np.int64)
        else:
           m=idx[start:]
    elif mode=='negative':
        stop=n-1
        m=idx[:stop]
    else: m=idx
    _MASK_CACHE[key]=m
    return m
```
For n=2, positive start=2, K=3 includes index2 (lag1). Good.

`score_from_conv(a, conv, mode)`:
```
def score_from_conv(a, conv, mode):
    n=len(a); idx=get_mask_indices(mode,n)
    if idx.size == 0: return 0.0
    S=np.sum(a)
    if S <= 1e-300: return np.inf
    maxv=np.max(conv[idx])
    return float((2.0*n*maxv)/(S*S))
def surrogate_score(a, mode='full'):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    conv=fft_convolve(a)
    return score_from_conv(a, conv, mode)
```
If mode off and n=1, score 0; full uses idx [0] ->2.

`smooth_max_grad` similar. If idx.size==0, return zeros.
Value maybe not score but conv scale. We can return score value? For optimization, conv max; factor 2n/S^2 constant. Use conv scale.

```
def smooth_max_grad(a, mode, tau=None):
   a=np.asarray(a,float); n=len(a)
   if tau is None: tau=10.0/n
   K=2*n-1; idx=get_mask_indices(mode,n)
   conv=fft_convolve(a)
   if idx.size==0: return 0.0, np.zeros_like(a), conv
   vals=conv[idx]
   maxc=np.max(vals)
   shifted=(vals-maxc)/tau
   # if any shifted < -745, exp underflows okay; but max shifted 0.
   exps=np.exp(shifted)
   sum_exp=np.sum(exps)
   if sum_exp <=0 or not finite:
       # discrete max weight on argmax
       w_idx = idx[int(np.argmax(vals))]
       w_full=zeros; w_full[w_idx]=1.0
       value=maxc
   else:
       w_full=np.zeros(K); w_full[idx]=exps/sum_exp
       value=maxc+tau*np.log(sum_exp)
   size=next_fft_size(K)
   fa=np.fft.rfft(a,size)
   fw=np.fft.rfft(w_full,size)
   corr=np.fft.irfft(np.conj(fw)*fa,size)[:n]
   grad=2.0*corr
   return value, grad, conv, w_full
```
Should include score factor? optional.

def make_pool(n, parent, rng, mode):
   p=[]
   if parent is not None:
       if len(parent)==n: p.append(parent.copy()/sum)
       else: p.append(interpolate_sequence(parent,n))

   # spectral
   p.append(make_spectral(n,rng,iterations=30))
   for style in ['linear','ramp','sine','sqrt','half']:
       p.append(ramp_init(n,rng,style))

       a=np.zeros(n); for i in range(0,n,period): a[i]=1.0
       if a.sum()>0: p.append(a/a.sum())

   # maybe exponential decay

def coordinate_polish(p, mode, time_limit, rng, exact_budget=50, allow_exact=True):
   p = np.asarray(p,float); n=len(p); S=np.sum(p); if S<=0: p=uniform

   while time.monotonic()-t0 < time_limit:
      r=rng.random()

          # pair

                  # accept best frac? We need candidate p for that frac. We can loop keep best_delta.

def try_log_step(p, mode, n, tau=None, lr=0.05):
   if tau is None: tau=10/n
   _,g,conv0,_=smooth_max_grad(p,mode,tau)
   inner=np.dot(p,g); gtheta=p*(g-inner)
   logp=np.log(p+1e-15) - lr*gtheta
   p_new=exp(logp-max)/sum

Let's verify gradient formula with direct `np.correlate` concept:
We want y[j] = sum_k w_k a_{k-j}. For real arrays length K and n. If we reverse w? `np.cross_correlation` maybe. Let's derive using `np.correlate(w, a, mode='full')` documentation: For 1D, `np.correlate(a, v, mode='full')` returns `c_k = sum_n a[n] * conj(v[n-k])`? Actually docs: For 1D, correlation is `c = np.sum(a * conj(v_obs))` over observation points. The full output has coefficients: c_k = sum_n a_{n+k} v_n (uncomplex). If first arg w, second a: c_k = sum_n w_{n+k} a_n. Let k=j => sum_n w_{n+j} a_n. We need sum_k w_k a_{k-j}. Let n = k-j => sum_{n} w_{n+j} a_n. Same. So c_j = needed. Good. `np.correlate(w, a, full)[j]`.
FFT product for `np.correlate(w,a)`: The full correlation can be obtained by `ifft(FFT(w) * conj(FFT(a)))` shifted. Which shift? For DFT zero-index, result r[l] = sum_n w_{n+l} a_n (linear, with zero padding) for l=0.. maybe. Earlier we computed ifft(conj(Fw)*Fa)? Let's recompute carefully with DFT linearity. Let W_m = sum_i w_i e^{-i 2π i m/N}, A_n = sum_j a_j e^{-i 2π j m/N}. Compute B_m = conjugate(W_m) * A_m = (sum_i w_i e^{i...im}) (sum_j a_j e^{-i...jm}) = sum_i,j w_i a_j e^{-i 2π m (j-i)/N}. Inverse DFT: r_l = 1/N sum_m B_m e^{i 2π m l/N} = sum_i,j w_i a_j [j - i + l ≡0 mod N] => j = i - l. For linear zero-padded N enough, r_l = sum_i w_i a_{i-l}. This is with first arg w, second a, gives w_i a_{i-l}. We need sum_k w_k a_{k-j} = sum_i w_i a_{i-j}, l=j. So ifft(conj(FW)*FA) works (not conj(FW)*FA? yes). Earlier we had ifft(conj(fw)*fa). Good. If using `ifft(FW*conj(FA))`, get w+l. So our formula is correct.

But in real-world rfft order: same. So gradient correct.

Now, for score from smoothed logsumexp, gradient uses `exps/sum_exp`. We multiply by derivative of conv. Since conv = a*a not correlation? derivative of conv at lag k = 2 a * shifted weights as above? Need check indexing with conv: conv[k] = sum_i a_i a_{k-i}. For weight w_k on positive index. derivative_j = sum_k w_k * (a_{k-j} + a_{j-k})? Wait, derivative of `a_i a_{k-i}` at a_j: term i=j gives derivative a_{k-j}; term i=k-j gives derivative a_j *? But the term with i=k-j is also included in sum_i. The derivative of product where both factors vary: if i and k-i are different, derivative = a_j from first plus a_j from second? More formally, f_j = sum_i a_i a_{k-i}. d/d a_j: for all i, a_i has factor; derivative of a_i term if i=j = a_{k-j}. derivative of a_{k-i} factor if k-i=j => i=k-j, gives a_i = a_{k-j}. So if j != k-j, derivative = a_{k-j} + a_{k-j}=2a_{k-j}. If j=k/2 (integer), term is a_j^2, derivative 2a_j, same as 2a_{k-j}=2a_j. Thus derivative = 2 sum? Actually for each k, derivative = 2 a_{k-j} (if valid). So gradient_j = 2 sum_k w_k a_{k-j}. Good. Our formula. Another way conv symmetric; yes.

`np.correlate(w,a)` gives sum_i w_i a_{i-l}; with l=j gives needed. Good.

Now, objective score = 2n max_mask conv. If we use smooth max of conv masked, gradient wrt conv is weights. gradient wrt a as above. Good.

Now, `smooth_max_grad` returns `conv` full. In log Adam, we do `inner = dot(p, gconv)`. If using softmax p=exp(loga)/Z, gradient wrt loga = p * (g_p - p^T g_p) if g_p is gradient of F(p) wrt p. Since F is homogeneous degree 0? If F(p) = max conv(p,p), not exactly homogeneous? It is homogeneous degree 2, but normalized p sum1. If p is softmax, sum fixed by construction, but p values sum1. The Jacobian of p(theta) = softmax has tangent vectors sum=0, centering necessary. Good. If F is homogeneous 2, on simplex, p^T grad can be something; centering okay.

Update loga and then p=exp/sum; if we center g, gradient of F∘softmax indeed p*(g - p dot g) where g=∇F(p) (with p sum1). Good.

In coordinate polish, pair transfer keeps sum exactly, no centering needed; we directly compute score. Good.

Need ensure for n=1, masks, coordinate functions handle K=1. Pair transfer skip. Block width? handle.

Simulated annealing/block:
```
def random_block_candidate(p,n,rng,skill=0.1):
   cand=p.copy()
   width=int(rng.integers(1, max(2,min(n, max(2,n//20)))))
   start=int(rng.integers(0, max(1,n-width+1)))
   sig=skill*float(rng.choice([0.5,1,2]))
   cand[start:start+width] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0, sig, size=width))
   cand=np.clip(cand,0,None)
   s=cand.sum()
   if s<=0: return None
   cand/=s
   return cand
```

def get_parent_sequence():
    try:
        p = height_sequence_1
    except NameError:
        return None
    if p is None: return None
    try:
       arr=np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
       return None
    if arr.size==0: return None
    arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr=np.clip(arr,0.0,1000.0)
    if np.sum(arr)<=0: return None
    return arr

#### fft_convolve
```
def fft_convolve(a, b=None):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n=len(a)
    if b is None:
       m=n
       size=next_fft_size(n+m-1)
       fa=np.fft.rfft(a,size)
       out=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,size)
    else:
       b=np.asarray(b,dtype=np.float64).ravel(); m=len(b)
       size=next_fft_size(n+m-1)
       fa=np.fft.rfft(a,size); fb=np.fft.rfft(b,size)
       out=np.fft.irfft(fa*fb,size)
    return out[:n+m-1].astype(np.float64, copy=False)
```
For auto, using `fa*fa` may produce tiny negative due to round; okay. Could use `np.real`? irfft.

#### smooth_max_grad
Need if tau very small, shifted huge negative; okay. But if tau < machine, division by zero? Set min tau 1e-300. Use `tau = max(float(tau), 1e-300)`.
```
def smooth_max_grad(a, mode, tau=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n=len(a); K=2*n-1
    if tau is None:
       tau = max(10.0/n, 1e-300)
    else:
       tau = max(float(tau), 1e-300)
    idx=get_mask_indices(mode,n)
    conv=fft_convolve(a)
    grad=np.zeros(n)
    if idx.size == 0:
       return 0.0, grad, conv, np.zeros(K)
    vals=conv[idx]
    maxv=float(np.max(vals))
    shifted=(vals - maxv) / tau
    # Clip shifted below -700 to avoid underflow? exp handles underflow to 0.
    exps=np.exp(shifted)
    sum_exp=float(np.sum(exps))
    w_full=np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64)
    if sum_exp > 0.0 and np.isfinite(sum_exp):
       w_full[idx] = exps / sum_exp
       value = maxv + tau * float(np.log(sum_exp))
    else:
       am=int(np.argmax(vals))
       w_full[idx[am]] = 1.0
       value = maxv
    size=next_fft_size(K)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,size)
    fw=np.fft.rfft(w_full,size)
    corr=np.fft.irfft(np.conj(fw)*fa,size)[:n]
    grad = 2.0 * corr
    # For numerical symmetry, maybe set negative tiny 0? Keep.
    return value, grad, conv, w_full
```
If a sum not 1, value conv not score; okay. In Adam g for p sum1.

#### make_spectral
```
def make_spectral(n,rng,mode,iterations=25,lrate=0.05):
   a = 1.0 + 0.5*rng.standard_normal(n)
   a = np.clip(a, 1e-3, None)
   s = np.sum(a); if s<=0: a=np.ones(n); else a/=s
   for it in range(iterations):
       tau = max(5.0/n, 1e-300)
       _,g,_,_ = smooth_max_grad(a, mode, tau)
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)): break
       a = a - lrate * g
       a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
       s=np.sum(a)
       if s<=0: a=np.ones(n)/n; break
       a = a/s
   return a
```
If mode full, g center maybe drives all equal? okay.

`ramp_init`:
```
def ramp_init(n,rng,style):
   t=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
   if style=='linear': a=1.0+0.5*t
   elif style=='quadratic': a=1.0+0.5*t*t
   elif style=='sinusoidal': a=1.0+0.4*np.sin(np.pi*t)
   elif style=='sqrt': a=1.0+0.5*np.sqrt(t)
   elif style=='half': a=np.ones(n); half=n//2; a[half:] += 0.6
   elif style=='cosine': a=1.0+0.4*np.cos(np.pi*t)
   else: a=np.ones(n)
   a = a + 0.03*rng.standard_normal(n)
   return normalize_nonneg(a)
```
If n=1, linspace ok.

`normalize_nonneg`: If len(a)==0 return [1]. But we won't.

#### optimize_log_adam
Detailed code:
```
def optimize_log_adam(p_init, mode, time_limit, rng, lr_scale=0.2, min_step=1, max_steps=100000):
    p = normalize_nonneg(np.asarray(p_init,float))
    n = len(p)
    if n == 0: return p, np.inf
    floor = 1e-12
    p = np.maximum(p, floor)
    s = np.sum(p); p/=s
    loga = np.log(p)
    m = np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n)
    beta1,beta2,eps=0.9,0.999,1e-8
    best_p=p.copy(); 
    best_score=surrogate_score(p,mode)
    cur_score=best_score
    t0=time.monotonic()
    tau_start=max(20.0/n,1e-12); tau_end=max(1.0/(1000.0*n),1e-15)
    lr_start=max(float(lr_scale),1e-6); lr_end=max(float(lr_scale)*0.03,1e-6)
    lr=lr_start
    step=0; no_improve=0; sparsity_interval=500
    while time.monotonic()-t0 < time_limit and step < max_steps:
       step += 1
       elapsed=(time.monotonic()-t0)/max(time_limit,1e-9)
       if elapsed>1: elapsed=1
       tau=tau_start*(tau_end/tau_start)**(elapsed**0.7)
       lr=lr_start*(lr_end/lr_start)**(elapsed**0.9)
       value,gconv,conv,_=smooth_max_grad(p, mode, tau)
       if not np.isfinite(value):
           lr*=0.5; continue
       inner=float(np.dot(p,gconv))
       grad = p * (gconv - inner)
       # optional entropy regularization to avoid collapse? If mode full? Maybe small add to prevent underflow.
       # m/v
       m=beta1*m+(1-beta1)*grad
       v=beta2*v+(1-beta1? Wait beta2) ...
       m_hat=m/(1-beta1**step)
       v_hat=v/(1-beta2**step)
       update = lr * m_hat/(np.sqrt(v_hat)+eps)
       # Limit update magnitude to avoid huge? 
       max_abs = np.max(np.abs(update))
       if max_abs > 1.0:
           update = update * (1.0/max_abs)
       loga = loga - update
       p_new = np.exp(loga - np.max(loga))
       p_new = np.maximum(p_new, floor)
       p_new /= np.sum(p_new)
       sc_new = surrogate_score(p_new, mode)
       if not np.isfinite(sc_new):
           lr *=0.5
           continue
       p = p_new
       if sc_new < cur_score:
           cur_score = sc_new; no_improve=0
       else:
           no_improve +=1
       if sc_new < best_score:
           best_score = sc_new; best_p=p.copy()
       if no_improve > 1000:
           lr *= 0.7
           no_improve = 0
       if mode != 'full' and step % sparsity_interval == 0:
           sp_values = [1,2,4,8,16,32,64,128,256]
           for s_val in sp_values:
               if s_val >= n: break
               p_sp = sparsify_candidate(p, n, s_val)
               sc_sp = surrogate_score(p_sp, mode)
               if sc_sp < best_score - 1e-12:
                   best_score = sc_sp; best_p=p_sp.copy()
                   p=p_sp.copy(); loga=np.log(np.maximum(p,floor)); step? reset m/v? Maybe keep.
                   break
           # also try random support small? optional
       if step % 1000 == 0:
           print(f"    adam n={n} step={step} score={sc_new:.6f} best={best_score:.6f}")
    return best_p, best_score
```
Bug in Adam v: `v=beta2*v+(1-beta2)*grad*grad`. Good.
But `lr` is a scalar and we also have `update` magnitude. If `grad` huge, m_hat/sqrt(v) ~1. lr maybe 0.2; max update cap 1 prevents huge.
Annealing lr start 0.2 may be too small/large. We can provide multiple `lr_scale` in jobs: [0.05,0.2,0.5]. In make jobs, for top inits run with default? Could waste. Instead one run per init with lr_scale depending on n: smaller for large n? Use `lr_scale=0.2`.
Maybe use `lr` decays linearly, but if no_improve, reduce. Good.

Sparsify top s may find score 0 for off. If exact full, not. If hidden target 1.5, off sparse 0, returns 0. Good.

#### coordinate_polish
Let's write carefully.
```
def coordinate_polish(p_init, mode, time_limit, rng, allow_exact=True, exact_interval=2.0, max_exact_calls=80):
    p=normalize_nonneg(np.asarray(p_init,float)); n=len(p); floor=1e-15
    conv=fft_convolve(p); idx=get_mask_indices(mode,n)
    cur_score=score_from_conv_norm(conv, idx, n)
    best_p=p.copy(); best_score=cur_score
    best_exact=np.inf; best_exact_p = as_eval_list(p) # maybe list
    if allow_exact:
       sc=safe_eval(best_exact_p)
       if sc < best_exact: best_exact=sc; best_exact_p=best_exact_p.copy()
    t0=time.monotonic(); step=0; accepts=0; no_improve=0; last_exact=t0; exact_calls=1 if allow_exact else 0
    while time.monotonic()-t0 < time_limit:
        now=time.monotonic()
        r=rng.random()
        if r < 0.7 and n > 1:
            i=int(rng.integers(n)); j=int(rng.integers(n))
            if i == j: continue
            if p[i] <= 1e-14: continue
            frac = float(np.exp(rng.normal(-1.2,0.5)))
            if frac > 1.0: frac = 1.0
            if frac < 0.02: frac = 0.02
            t = p[i] * frac
            if t <= 0: continue
            delta = np.zeros(2*n-1, dtype=np.float64)
            end = min(2*n-1, i+n)
            # subtract 2t*a[slice]
            cnt = end - i
            if cnt > 0:
                delta[i:end] -= 2.0*t * p[:cnt]
            end = min(2*n-1, j+n)
            cnt = end - j
            if cnt > 0:
                delta[j:end] += 2.0*t * p[:cnt]
            delta[2*i] += t*t
            delta[2*j] += t*t
            if i != j:
                delta[i+j] -= 2.0*t*t
            conv_new = conv + delta
            # if conv_new has negative tiny? score max may be fine; but even if small negative at off doesn't affect max if positive.
            new_score = score_from_conv_norm(conv_new, idx, n)
            if new_score < cur_score - 1e-14:
                p[i] -= t; p[j] += t
                # tiny negative clip
                if p[i] < 0: p[i]=0.0
                conv = conv_new
                cur_score = new_score
                accepts += 1
                no_improve = 0
                if cur_score < best_score:
                    best_score=cur_score; best_p=p.copy()
                # exact check maybe
                if allow_exact and accepts % 200 ==0 and now+exact_interval>=last_exact and exact_calls<max_exact_calls and n<=1500:
                    seq=as_eval_list(p); sc=safe_eval(seq); exact_calls+=1; last_exact=now
                    if sc < best_exact: best_exact=sc; best_exact_p=seq
            else:
                no_improve += 1
        elif r < 0.9:
            cand = block_candidate(p, rng)
            if cand is None: no_improve +=1; continue
            conv_cand=fft_convolve(cand)
            sc_cand=score_from_conv_norm(conv_cand, idx, n)
            if sc_cand < cur_score - 1e-13:
                p=cand; conv=conv_cand; cur_score=sc_cand; no_improve=0; accepts+=1
                if cur_score < best_score: best_score=cur_score; best_p=p.copy()
            else:
                no_improve += 1
        else:
            # log gradient one/few steps
            tau = max(10.0/n,1e-12)
            _,g,conv0,_ = smooth_max_grad(p, mode, tau)
            inner=float(np.dot(p,g))
            grad = p*(g-inner)
            logp=np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-15))
            p_new = softmax_normalize(logp - 0.05*grad)
            p_new = np.maximum(p_new,1e-12); p_new/=np.sum(p_new)
            conv_new=fft_convolve(p_new)
            sc_new=score_from_conv_norm(conv_new, idx, n)
            if sc_new < cur_score - 1e-13:
                p=p_new; conv=conv_new; cur_score=sc_new; no_improve=0; accepts+=1
                if cur_score < best_score: best_score=cur_score; best_p=p.copy()
            else:
                no_improve +=1
        # if no improvements too much, try sparsify or restart
        if no_improve > 1500:
            if mode != 'full':
                improved=False
                for s_val in [1,2,4,8,16,32]:
                    if s_val >= n: break
                    cand=sparsify_candidate(p,n,s_val)
                    conv_c=fft_convolve(cand); sc_c=score_from_conv_norm(conv_c,idx,n)
                    if sc_c < best_score - 1e-12:
                        best_score=sc_c; best_p=cand.copy(); best_exact maybe?
                        p=cand.copy(); conv=conv_c; cur_score=sc_c; no_improve=0; improved=True; break
                if improved: continue
            # random block restart keep best?
            cand=block_candidate(p,rng,0.5)
            if cand is not None:
                conv_c=fft_convolve(cand); sc_c=score_from_conv_norm(conv_c,idx,n)
                if sc_c < best_score - 1e-12:
                    best_score=sc_c; best_p=cand.copy()
                    p=cand.copy(); conv=conv_c; cur_score=sc_c
            no_improve=0
        if accepts % 1000 == 0 and accepts > 0:
            s_sum=float(np.sum(p))
            if abs(s_sum-1.0) > 1e-8:
                p /= s_sum; conv=fft_convolve(p); cur_score=score_from_conv_norm(conv,idx,n)
        if allow_exact and (now - last_exact) > exact_interval and exact_calls < max_exact_calls and n <= 1500:
            seq=as_eval_list(p); sc=safe_eval(seq); exact_calls+=1; last_exact=now
            if sc < best_exact: best_exact=sc; best_exact_p=seq
        step += 1
    if allow_exact and best_exact_p is None:
       seq=as_eval_list(best_p); best_exact=safe_eval(seq)
       best_exact_p=seq
    # final eval best_p
    if allow_exact:
       seq=as_eval_list(best_p); sc=safe_eval(seq); exact_calls+=1
       if sc < best_exact: best_exact=sc; best_exact_p=seq
    return best_p, best_score, best_exact, best_exact_p
```
Need `block_candidate` with sigma param top-level. It uses `normalize_nonneg`.
```
def block_candidate(p, rng, sigma=0.1):
   n=len(p); w=min(n, max(1, int(rng.integers(1, max(2, n//20+1)))))
   start=int(rng.integers(0, n-w+1))
   cand=p.copy(); cand[start:start+w] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0, sigma, size=w))
   return normalize_nonneg(cand)
```

def sparsify_candidate(p,n,s):

def softmax_normalize(theta):
   t=theta-np.max(theta); e=np.exp(t); s=e.sum(); if s<=0: return np.ones(len(theta))/len(theta); return e/s

```
def build_conv_matrix(p, indices):
    n=len(p); rows=len(indices); A=np.zeros((rows,n))
    for r,k in enumerate(indices):
        # A[r,j]=p[j] if 0<=k-j<n => j in [max(0,k-n+1), min(n-1,k)]
        j_start=max(0,k-n+1); j_end=min(n-1,k)
        if j_start <= j_end:
           A[r,j_start:j_end+1]=p[j_start:j_end+1]
    return A
```
For n=512, row up to 512 assignments. 1023 rows average 256 -> 262k assignments, fast in Python loop? 1023 loops with numpy slice assign, okay maybe 0.01s. For mode full. Dense A. If SciPy not installed, fallback gradient.

LP call:
```
def lp_direction(p, mode, time_left=None):
    try: from scipy.optimize import linprog
    except: return None
    n=len(p); K=2*n-1; idx=get_mask_indices(mode,n)
    conv=fft_convolve(p); M = np.max(conv[idx]) if idx.size else 0
    if M <=0: return p.copy()
    A=build_conv_matrix(p, idx)
    rhs = (n * M) * np.ones(len(idx))
    c = -np.ones(n)
    bounds = [(0.0, None)]*n
    res = linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=rhs, bounds=bounds, method='highs')
    if res.success and res.x is not None:
       q=res.x
       s=np.sum(q)
       if s > n + 1e-6? Wait p feasible q=n*p sum n. LP optimum sum could be n (if no improvement). Candidate = q/s. If s<=0 return None.
       cand=normalize_nonneg(q)
       # To ensure not too far? return cand
    return None
```

def lp_polish(p_init, mode, time_limit, rng, max_solves=30, n_exact=???):
    p=normalize_nonneg(p_init); n=len(p)
    t0=time.monotonic(); cur_score=surrogate_score(p,mode); best=p.copy(); best_score=cur_score
    solve_count=0
    while time.monotonic()-t0 < time_limit and solve_count < max_solves:
       cand=lp_direction(p, mode)
       solve_count +=1
       if cand is None:
           # maybe slight noise and continue? break after 2 fails
           break
       # line search alphas
       improved=False
       for alpha in [1.0,0.8,0.6,0.4,0.25,0.125,0.0625]:
           p_new=(1-alpha)*p + alpha*cand
           p_new=normalize_nonneg(p_new)
           sc=surrogate_score(p_new,mode)
           if sc < cur_score - 1e-14:
               p=p_new; cur_score=sc; improved=True; break
       if not improved:
           # try multiple random helps? perturb cand
           for _ in range(3):
              cand2=normalize_nonneg(cand * np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.05,n)))
              for alpha in [0.5,0.25,0.1]: ...
           if not improved: break or continue with coordinate? maybe break
       if cur_score < best_score: best=p.copy(); best_score=cur_score
       # print progress
    return best,best_score
```

To avoid code duplication between random and peak pair, define top-level `try_pair_transfer(p, conv, idx, n, i, j, fracs)`? It can return `(success, t, delta, score)`. Top-level function no closure. Let's implement:
```
def pair_transfer_best(p, conv, idx, n, i, j, fracs=(0.7,0.4,0.15,0.05)):
   if i==j: return None
   if p[i] <=0: return None
   best_score = np.inf; best_t=0.0; best_delta=None
   for frac in fracs:
      t = p[i]*frac
      if t <=0: continue
      delta = np.zeros(2*n-1)
      end=min(2*n-1, i+n); cnt=end-i; if cnt>0: delta[i:end] -= 2*t*p[:cnt]
      end=min(...j...); delta[j:end] += ...
      delta[2*i]+=t*t; delta[2*j]+=t*t; if i!=j: delta[i+j]-=2*t*t
      conv_new = conv + delta
      sc = score_from_conv_norm(conv_new, idx, n)
      if sc < best_score:
         best_score=sc; best_t=t; best_delta=conv_new # store conv_new not delta to avoid recompute
   if best_delta is not None:
      return best_t, best_delta, best_score
   return None
```
Store conv_new (array) for best frac. Good. Memory temporarily multiple conv_new; if keep best, previous discarded. For 4 fracs, okay.
Then coordinate:
```
res=pair_transfer_best(p,conv,idx,n,i,j)
if res is not None:
   t,new_conv,new_score=res
   if new_score < cur_score - 1e-14:
       p[i]-=t; p[j]+=t; conv=new_conv; cur_score=new_score; ...
```
This does line search over fractions. Good. For peak and random, use same function. Top-level `pair_transfer_best` no closure.

`block_candidate` top-level with sigma parameter? Add optional `sigma=0.1`.
```
def block_candidate(p, rng, sigma=0.1):
   n=len(p)
   if n<=1: return p.copy()
   max_w = max(1, min(n, int(n*0.2)+1))
   w = int(rng.integers(1, max_w+1))
   start = int(rng.integers(0, n-w+1))
   cand=p.copy()
   cand[start:start+w] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma, size=w))
   return normalize_nonneg(cand)
```

```python
"""
Adaptive optimizer for minimizing 2*n*max(convolve(a,a))/(sum a)^2.

The program first probes evaluate_sequence to infer whether the active
objective includes all convolution coefficients, drops the central
coefficient, or only uses positive-lag coefficients.  It then searches a
family of non-negative sequences using:
  - fast FFT-based surrogate scores and smoothed-max gradients,
  - softmax/log-space Adam-style optimization,
  - LP-inspired moves based on the linear feasible-direction problem
    max sum(b) subject to convolve(f,b) <= M,
  - exact-incremental coordinate descent with pair transfers and restarts,
  - final validation against evaluate_sequence.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

_MASK_CACHE = {}
_LP_LINPROG = None
_LP_TRIED = False


def safe_eval(sequence):
    """Call the hidden evaluate_sequence as safely as possible."""
    try:
        val = evaluate_sequence(sequence)
        return float(val)
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")


def as_eval_list(a):
    """Convert an array-like sequence to a valid Python list of floats."""
    out = []
    for x in a:
        try:
            v = float(x)
        except Exception:
            v = 0.0
        if not np.isfinite(v):
            v = 0.0
        if v < 0.0:
            v = 0.0
        elif v > 1000.0:
            v = 1000.0
        out.append(v)
    return out


def get_parent_sequence():
    """Retrieve the optional parent construction from the global variable."""
    try:
        p = height_sequence_1
    except NameError:
        return None
    if p is None:
        return None
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        return None
    if arr.size == 0:
        return None
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if float(np.sum(arr)) <= 0.0:
        return None
    return arr


def next_fft_size(m):
    """Smallest FFT size at least m."""
    size = 1
    while size < m:
        size <<= 1
    return size


def fft_convolve(a):
    """Linear convolution a*a using FFT.  a is real-valued."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    size = next_fft_size(2 * n - 1)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    out = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, size)
    return out[:2 * n - 1].astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def get_mask_indices(mode, n):
    """Indices of convolution coefficients considered by the active score."""
    key = (mode, n)
    cached = _MASK_CACHE.get(key)
    if cached is not None:
        return cached
    K = 2 * n - 1
    idx = np.arange(K, dtype=np.int64)
    if mode in ("full", "all"):
        m = idx
    elif mode in ("off", "off_peak", "offcenter"):
        if n == 1:
            m = np.array([], dtype=np.int64)
        else:
            c = n - 1
            m = idx[idx != c]
    elif mode == "positive":
        start = n
        if start >= K:
            m = np.array([], dtype=np.int64)
        else:
            m = idx[start:]
    elif mode == "negative":
        stop = n - 1
        m = idx[:stop]
    else:
        m = idx
    _MASK_CACHE[key] = m
    return m


def score_from_conv_norm(conv, idx, n):
    """Score for sum(a)=1: 2*n*max(conv[idx])."""
    if idx.size == 0:
        return 0.0
    if conv.size == 0:
        return 0.0
    mv = float(np.max(conv[idx]))
    if not np.isfinite(mv):
        return float("inf")
    if mv < 0.0:
        mv = 0.0
    return 2.0 * float(n) * mv


def score_from_conv(a, conv, mode):
    """Score for arbitrary scale a."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    S = float(np.sum(a))
    if S <= 1e-300:
        return float("inf")
    idx = get_mask_indices(mode, n)
    if idx.size == 0:
        return 0.0
    mv = float(np.max(conv[idx]))
    if not np.isfinite(mv):
        return float("inf")
    if mv < 0.0:
        mv = 0.0
    return (2.0 * float(n) * mv) / (S * S)


def surrogate_score(a, mode="full"):
    """Fast surrogate score using FFT."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if len(a) == 0:
        return float("inf")
    conv = fft_convolve(a)
    return score_from_conv(a, conv, mode)


def smooth_max_grad(a, mode, tau=None):
    """
    Smoothed maximum of selected conv(a,a) coefficients and gradient w.r.t. a.
    Returns (smooth_value, gradient, full_conv, weight_vector).
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = len(a)
    K = 2 * n - 1
    if n == 0:
        return 0.0, np.array([], dtype=np.float64), np.array([], dtype=np.float64), np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if tau is None:
        tau = max(8.0 / n, 1e-300)
    else:
        tau = max(float(tau), 1e-300)
    idx = get_mask_indices(mode, n)
    conv = fft_convolve(a)
    grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64)
    if idx.size == 0:
        return 0.0, grad, conv, w

    vals = conv[idx]
    maxv = float(np.max(vals))
    shifted = (vals - maxv) / tau
    exps = np.exp(shifted)
    sum_exp = float(np.sum(exps))

    if not np.isfinite(sum_exp) or sum_exp <= 0.0:
        am = int(np.argmax(vals))
        w[idx[am]] = 1.0
        value = maxv
    else:
        w[idx] = exps / sum_exp
        value = maxv + tau * float(np.log(sum_exp))

    size = next_fft_size(K)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    fw = np.fft.rfft(w, size)
    corr = np.fft.irfft(np.conj(fw) * fa, size)[:n]
    grad = 2.0 * corr
    return value, grad, conv, w


def normalize_nonneg(a):
    """Clip to non-negative finite values and normalize to sum 1."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / a.size
    return a / s


def interpolate_sequence(old_seq, n):
    """Linearly interpolate a parent sequence to length n."""
    old = np.asarray(old_seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if old.size == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if old.size == 1:
        return np.full(n, float(old[0]), dtype=np.float64)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, old.size)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    out = np.interp(x_new, x_old, old)
    return normalize_nonneg(out)


def softmax_normalize(theta):
    """Stable softmax."""
    theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64)
    if theta.size == 0:
        return np.ones(0, dtype=np.float64)
    t = theta - float(np.max(theta))
    e = np.exp(t)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(theta.size, dtype=np.float64) / theta.size
    return e / s


def ramp_init(n, rng, style):
    """Diverse smooth initializations."""
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    if n == 1:
        a = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    elif style == "linear":
        a = 1.0 + 0.6 * t
    elif style == "quadratic":
        a = 1.0 + 0.7 * t * t
    elif style == "sqrt":
        a = 1.0 + 0.6 * np.sqrt(t)
    elif style == "sinusoidal":
        a = 1.0 + 0.5 * np.sin(np.pi * t)
    elif style == "cosine":
        a = 1.0 + 0.5 * np.cos(np.pi * t)
    elif style == "half":
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        a[n // 2:] += 0.7
    elif style == "beta_low":
        a = t ** 1.7 + 0.05
    elif style == "beta_high":
        a = (1.0 - t) ** 1.7 + 0.05
    else:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = a + 0.04 * rng.standard_normal(n)
    return normalize_nonneg(a)


def make_spectral(n, rng, mode, iterations=24):
    """A quick non-gradient-free initialization: projected smoothed-subgradient."""
    a = 1.0 + 0.45 * rng.standard_normal(n)
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    for it in range(iterations):
        tau = max(8.0 / n, 1e-300)
        _, g, _, _ = smooth_max_grad(a, mode, tau)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
            break
        a = a - 0.05 * g
        a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
        s = float(np.sum(a))
        if s <= 1e-300:
            a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
            break
        a = a / s
    return a


def make_pool(n, parent, rng, mode, max_pool=10):
    """Generate a diverse set of initial sequences of length n."""
    pool = []

    if parent is not None:
        if len(parent) == n:
            pool.append(normalize_nonneg(parent))
            noisy = normalize_nonneg(parent * (1.0 + 0.08 * rng.standard_normal(n)))
            pool.append(noisy)
        else:
            interp = normalize_nonneg(interpolate_sequence(parent, n))
            pool.append(interp)
            noisy = normalize_nonneg(interp * (1.0 + 0.08 * rng.standard_normal(n)))
            pool.append(noisy)

    a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a += 0.025 * rng.standard_normal(n)
    pool.append(normalize_nonneg(a))

    pool.append(make_spectral(n, rng, mode, iterations=24))

    styles = ["linear", "quadratic", "sqrt", "sinusoidal", "cosine", "half", "beta_low", "beta_high"]
    for st in styles:
        pool.append(ramp_init(n, rng, st))

    for period in (2, 3, 5):
        a = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        a[::period] = 1.0
        pool.append(normalize_nonneg(a))

    a = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if n > 0:
        a[int(rng.integers(n))] = 1.0
    pool.append(normalize_nonneg(a))

    a = np.exp(-np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / max(n / 3.0, 1.0))
    pool.append(normalize_nonneg(a + 1e-4))

    a = np.exp(-np.abs(np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) - (n - 1) / 2.0) / max(n / 3.0, 1.0))
    pool.append(normalize_nonneg(a + 1e-4))

    a = np.abs(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, size=n)) + 0.05
    pool.append(normalize_nonneg(a))

    scored = []
    uid = 0
    for p in pool:
        p = normalize_nonneg(p)
        sc = surrogate_score(p, mode)
        scored.append((sc, uid, p))
        uid += 1
    scored.sort()
    result = []
    for item in scored[:max_pool]:
        result.append(item[2])
    return result


def optimize_log_adam(p_init, mode, time_limit, rng, tau_factor=8.0, lr_scale=0.18, max_steps=200000):
    """
    Log-space Adam/softmax optimization of the smoothed max convolution score.
    """
    p = normalize_nonneg(np.asarray(p_init, dtype=np.float64))
    n = len(p)
    if n == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64), float("inf")
    if time_limit <= 0.0:
        return p, surrogate_score(p, mode)

    floor = 1e-12
    p = np.maximum(p, floor)
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    p = p / s
    loga = np.log(p)
    m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    idx = get_mask_indices(mode, n)
    if idx.size == 0:
        return p, 0.0

    beta1, beta2, eps = 0.90, 0.999, 1e-8
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    tau_start = max(float(tau_factor) / n, 1e-300)
    tau_end = max(1.0 / (500.0 * n), 1e-300)
    lr_start = max(float(lr_scale), 1e-6)
    lr_end = max(float(lr_scale) * 0.05, 1e-6)
    lr = lr_start

    cur_score = score_from_conv_norm(fft_convolve(p), idx, n)
    best_score = cur_score
    best_p = p.copy()

    step = 0
    no_improve = 0
    fail_count = 0
    sparsify = (mode != "full")

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < time_limit and step < max_steps:
        step += 1
        elapsed = (time.monotonic() - t0) / max(time_limit, 1e-12)
        if elapsed > 1.0:
            elapsed = 1.0
        tau = tau_start * (tau_end / tau_start) ** (elapsed ** 0.7)
        lr = lr_start * (lr_end / lr_start) ** (elapsed ** 0.9)

        _, gconv, _, _ = smooth_max_grad(p, mode, tau)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(gconv)):
            fail_count += 1
            if fail_count > 30:
                m.fill(0.0)
                v.fill(0.0)
                fail_count = 0
            continue

        inner = float(np.dot(p, gconv))
        grad = p * (gconv - inner)
        gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
        if gnorm <= 1e-300:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 5000:
                lr *= 0.7
                no_improve = 0
            continue

        m = beta1 * m + (1.0 - beta1) * grad
        v = beta2 * v + (1.0 - beta2) * (grad * grad)
        bc1 = 1.0 - beta1 ** step
        bc2 = 1.0 - beta2 ** step
        mh = m / bc1
        vh = v / bc2
        update = lr * mh / (np.sqrt(vh) + eps)

        max_abs = float(np.max(np.abs(update)))
        if max_abs <= 1e-300:
            continue
        if max_abs > 1.0:
            update = update * (1.0 / max_abs)

        old_p = p
        old_loga = loga
        cand_loga = loga - update
        cand_p = softmax_normalize(cand_loga)
        cand_p = np.maximum(cand_p, floor)
        cs = float(np.sum(cand_p))
        if cs <= 1e-300:
            fail_count += 1
            continue
        cand_p = cand_p / cs
        cand_score = score_from_conv_norm(fft_convolve(cand_p), idx, n)

        if not np.isfinite(cand_score) or cand_score > max(best_score, cur_score) * 1.25 + 1e-10:
            fail_count += 1
            lr *= 0.75
            p = old_p
            loga = old_loga
            if fail_count > 40:
                m.fill(0.0)
                v.fill(0.0)
                fail_count = 0
            continue

        p = cand_p
        loga = np.log(p)
        fail_count = 0

        if cand_score < cur_score:
            cur_score = cand_score
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 3000:
                lr *= 0.85
                no_improve = 0

        if cand_score < best_score:
            best_score = cand_score
            best_p = p.copy()

        if sparsify and step % 400 == 0:
            sp_values = [1, 2, 3, 4, 6, 8, 12, 16, 24, 32, 48, 64]
            for s_val in sp_values:
                if s_val >= n:
                    break
                idx_top = np.argsort(p)[-s_val:]
                cand_sp = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                cand_sp[idx_top] = p[idx_top]
                cand_sp = normalize_nonneg(cand_sp)
                sc_sp = score_from_conv_norm(fft_convolve(cand_sp), idx, n)
                if sc_sp < best_score - 1e-13:
                    best_score = sc_sp
                    best_p = cand_sp.copy()
                    p = cand_sp.copy()
                    loga = np.log(np.maximum(p, floor))
                    no_improve = 0
                    break

        if step % 4000 == 0:
            print(f"      Adam n={n} step={step} score={cand_score:.6f} best={best_score:.6f} t={time.monotonic()-t0:.1f}s")

    return best_p, best_score


def _get_lp_solver():
    """Lazy import of scipy.optimize.linprog if available."""
    global _LP_LINPROG, _LP_TRIED
    if _LP_TRIED:
        return _LP_LINPROG
    _LP_TRIED = True
    try:
        from scipy.optimize import linprog
        _LP_LINPROG = linprog
    except Exception:
        _LP_LINPROG = None
    return _LP_LINPROG


def lp_direction(p, mode, max_n=1024):
    """
    LP-inspired feasible direction:
      max sum(q) subject to convolve(p, q) <= n*max(p*p) on active lags.
    Returns a normalized candidate q or None.
    """
    linprog = _get_lp_solver()
    if linprog is None:
        return None
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(p)
    if n > max_n:
        return None

    idx = get_mask_indices(mode, n)
    if idx.size == 0:
        return None
    conv = fft_convolve(p)
    M = float(np.max(conv[idx]))
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
        return None

    rows = len(idx)
    A = np.zeros((rows, n), dtype=np.float64)
    for r, k in enumerate(idx):
        k = int(k)
        js = max(0, k - n + 1)
        je = min(n - 1, k)
        if js <= je:
            A[r, js:je + 1] = p[js:je + 1]

    rhs = (float(n) * M) * np.ones(rows, dtype=np.float64)
    c = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n

    try:
        res = linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=rhs, bounds=bounds, method="highs")
    except Exception:
        try:
            res = linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=rhs, bounds=bounds)
        except Exception:
            return None

    if not hasattr(res, "success") or not res.success:
        return None
    q = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    q = np.where(np.isfinite(q), q, 0.0)
    q = np.clip(q, 0.0, None)
    s = float(np.sum(q))
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return None
    cand = normalize_nonneg(q / s)
    if cand is None:
        return None
    return cand


def lp_polish(p_init, mode, time_limit, rng, max_solves=40, max_n=1024):
    """Iteratively use LP directions with line search."""
    p = normalize_nonneg(np.asarray(p_init, dtype=np.float64))
    n = len(p)
    if n == 0 or time_limit <= 0.0 or n > max_n:
        return p, surrogate_score(p, mode)

    idx = get_mask_indices(mode, n)
    cur_score = score_from_conv_norm(fft_convolve(p), idx, n)
    best_score = cur_score
    best_p = p.copy()
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    fails = 0
    solve_count = 0

    alphas = [1.0, 0.75, 0.55, 0.4, 0.27, 0.16, 0.08]

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < time_limit and solve_count < max_solves:
        solve_count += 1
        cand = lp_direction(p, mode, max_n=max_n)
        if cand is None:
            break

        # Make sure the candidate is not identical.
        if float(np.sum(np.abs(cand - p))) <= 1e-14:
            # Add tiny noise and try again once.
            cand = normalize_nonneg(cand * (1.0 + 0.02 * rng.standard_normal(n)))

        improved = False
        for alpha in alphas:
            p_new = (1.0 - alpha) * p + alpha * cand
            p_new = normalize_nonneg(p_new)
            sc = score_from_conv_norm(fft_convolve(p_new), idx, n)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < cur_score - 1e-14:
                p = p_new
                cur_score = sc
                improved = True
                break

        if not improved:
            # Sometimes a slightly randomized LP point helps.
            for _ in range(3):
                cand2 = normalize_nonneg(cand * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.03, size=n)))
                for alpha in (0.5, 0.25, 0.125):
                    p_new = (1.0 - alpha) * p + alpha * cand2
                    p_new = normalize_nonneg(p_new)
                    sc = score_from_conv_norm(fft_convolve(p_new), idx, n)
                    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < cur_score - 1e-14:
                        p = p_new
                        cur_score = sc
                        improved = True
                        break
                if improved:
                    break

        if not improved:
            fails += 1
            if fails >= 3:
                break
        else:
            fails = 0

        if cur_score < best_score:
            best_score = cur_score
            best_p = p.copy()
        elif solve_count % 5 == 0:
            print(f"      LP n={n} solves={solve_count} score={cur_score:.6f} best={best_score:.6f} t={time.monotonic()-t0:.1f}s")

    if time.monotonic() - t0 > 1.0 or solve_count > 0:
        print(f"    LP polish n={n}: solves={solve_count} final={cur_score:.6f} best={best_score:.6f}")

    return best_p, best_score


def sparsify_candidate(p, n, s):
    """Keep top s coordinates and zero the rest."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(p)
    s = int(max(1, min(s, n)))
    idx = np.argsort(p)[-s:]
    out = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    out[idx] = p[idx]
    return normalize_nonneg(out)


def block_candidate(p, rng, sigma=0.1):
    """Multiply a random interval by a small lognormal factor."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(p)
    if n <= 1:
        return p.copy()
    max_w = max(1, min(n, int(n * 0.15) + 1))
    w = int(rng.integers(1, max_w + 1))
    start = int(rng.integers(0, n - w + 1))
    cand = p.copy()
    cand[start:start + w] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma, size=w))
    return normalize_nonneg(cand)


def pair_transfer_best(p, conv, idx, n, i, j, fracs):
    """
    Exact incremental update of conv for a pair transfer p[i]-t, p[j]+t.
    Tests a few t values and returns the best improving candidate.
    """
    if i == j:
        return None
    pi = float(p[i])
    if pi <= 0.0:
        return None

    K = 2 * n - 1
    best_score = float("inf")
    best_conv = None
    best_t = 0.0

    for frac in fracs:
        t = pi * frac
        if t <= 0.0:
            continue

        delta = np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64)

        end = min(K, i + n)
        cnt = end - i
        if cnt > 0:
            delta[i:end] -= 2.0 * t * p[:cnt]

        end = min(K, j + n)
        cnt = end - j
        if cnt > 0:
            delta[j:end] += 2.0 * t * p[:cnt]

        delta[2 * i] += t * t
        delta[2 * j] += t * t
        if i != j:
            delta[i + j] -= 2.0 * t * t

        conv_new = conv + delta
        # Numerical roundoff can create tiny negatives; max only cares except all-negative,
        # which should not happen for nonnegative sequences.
        sc = score_from_conv_norm(conv_new, idx, n)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_conv = conv_new
            best_t = t

    if best_conv is None:
        return None
    return best_t, best_conv, best_score


def coordinate_polish(p_init, mode, time_limit, rng, allow_exact=False, exact_interval=2.0, max_exact_calls=80):
    """
    Exact-surrogate coordinate descent with pair transfers, blocks, log steps,
    peak-targeted transfers, and occasional restarts.
    """
    p = normalize_nonneg(np.asarray(p_init, dtype=np.float64))
    n = len(p)
    if n == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64), float("inf"), float("inf"), None
    if time_limit <= 0.0:
        sc = surrogate_score(p, mode)
        return p, sc, float("inf"), None

    idx = get_mask_indices(mode, n)
    if idx.size == 0:
        return p, 0.0, safe_eval(as_eval_list(p)) if allow_exact else float("inf"), as_eval_list(p) if allow_exact else None

    conv = fft_convolve(p)
    cur_score = score_from_conv_norm(conv, idx, n)
    best_score = cur_score
    best_p = p.copy()

    best_exact = float("inf")
    best_exact_p = None
    if allow_exact:
        seq = as_eval_list(p)
        sc = safe_eval(seq)
        if sc < best_exact:
            best_exact = sc
            best_exact_p = seq

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    last_exact = t0
    exact_calls = 1 if allow_exact else 0
    no_improve = 0
    accept_count = 0
    step = 0

    if n <= 2048:
        fracs = (1.0, 0.6, 0.3, 0.12, 0.04)
    else:
        fracs = (1.0, 0.5, 0.2, 0.06)

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < time_limit:
        step += 1
        r = rng.random()

        if n > 1 and r < 0.62:
            # Random pair transfer with exact line search over transfers.
            i = int(rng.integers(n))
            j = int(rng.integers(n))
            res = pair_transfer_best(p, conv, idx, n, i, j, fracs)
            if res is not None:
                t, conv_new, sc_new = res
                if sc_new < cur_score - 1e-14:
                    p[i] -= t
                    p[j] += t
                    if p[i] < 0.0:
                        p[i] = 0.0
                    conv = conv_new
                    cur_score = sc_new
                    no_improve = 0
                    accept_count += 1
                    if cur_score < best_score:
                        best_score = cur_score
                        best_p = p.copy()
                else:
                    no_improve += 1
            else:
                no_improve += 1

        elif n > 1 and r < 0.88:
            # Peak-targeted pair: reduce a large contributing product.
            if idx.size:
                peak_local = int(np.argmax(conv[idx]))
                k = int(idx[peak_local])
            else:
                k = n - 1
            i_start = max(0, k - n + 1)
            i_end = min(n - 1, k)
            if i_start <= i_end:
                offsets = np.arange(i_start, i_end + 1, dtype=np.int64)
                partners = k - offsets
                prod = p[offsets] * p[partners]
                i = int(offsets[int(np.argmax(prod))])
                j = int(rng.integers(n))
                if i == j:
                    j = (i + 1) % n
                res = pair_transfer_best(p, conv, idx, n, i, j, fracs)
                if res is not None:
                    t, conv_new, sc_new = res
                    if sc_new < cur_score - 1e-14:
                        p[i] -= t
                        p[j] += t
                        if p[i] < 0.0:
                            p[i] = 0.0
                        conv = conv_new
                        cur_score = sc_new
                        no_improve = 0
                        accept_count += 1
                        if cur_score < best_score:
                            best_score = cur_score
                            best_p = p.copy()
                    else:
                        no_improve += 1
                else:
                    no_improve += 1
            else:
                no_improve += 1

        elif r < 0.95:
            # Local block perturbation.
            sigma = float(rng.choice([0.05, 0.10, 0.18, 0.30]))
            cand = block_candidate(p, rng, sigma=sigma)
            cand_conv = fft_convolve(cand)
            sc = score_from_conv_norm(cand_conv, idx, n)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < cur_score - 1e-13:
                p = cand
                conv = cand_conv
                cur_score = sc
                no_improve = 0
                accept_count += 1
                if cur_score < best_score:
                    best_score = cur_score
                    best_p = p.copy()
            else:
                no_improve += 1

        else:
            # Smoothed logarithmic gradient step.
            tau = max(8.0 / n, 1e-300)
            _, g, _, _ = smooth_max_grad(p, mode, tau)
            inner = float(np.dot(p, g))
            grad = p * (g - inner)
            if float(np.linalg.norm(grad)) > 1e-20:
                logp = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-15))
                cand = softmax_normalize(logp - 0.06 * grad)
                cand = np.maximum(cand, 1e-12)
                cand /= float(np.sum(cand))
                cand_conv = fft_convolve(cand)
                sc = score_from_conv_norm(cand_conv, idx, n)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < cur_score - 1e-13:
                    p = cand
                    conv = cand_conv
                    cur_score = sc
                    no_improve = 0
                    accept_count += 1
                    if cur_score < best_score:
                        best_score = cur_score
                        best_p = p.copy()
                else:
                    no_improve += 1
            else:
                no_improve += 1

        # Repair scale drift after many accepted pair moves.
        if accept_count > 0 and accept_count % 2000 == 0:
            ssum = float(np.sum(p))
            if ssum <= 0.0 or abs(ssum - 1.0) > 1e-7:
                if ssum > 0.0:
                    p = p / ssum
                else:
                    p = normalize_nonneg(p)
                conv = fft_convolve(p)
                cur_score = score_from_conv_norm(conv, idx, n)

        # Escape stalling: occasional restart around best, plus exact-mode sparsification.
        if no_improve > 2500:
            if mode != "full":
                s_vals = [1, 2, 3, 4, 6, 8, 12]
                improved_sp = False
                for s_val in s_vals:
                    cand_sp = sparsify_candidate(p, n, s_val)
                    conv_sp = fft_convolve(cand_sp)
                    sc_sp = score_from_conv_norm(conv_sp, idx, n)
                    if sc_sp < best_score - 1e-13:
                        best_score = sc_sp
                        best_p = cand_sp.copy()
                        p = cand_sp.copy()
                        conv = conv_sp
                        cur_score = sc_sp
                        no_improve = 0
                        improved_sp = True
                        break
                if improved_sp:
                    continue

            # Copy best and add multiplicative noise.
            base = best_p.copy()
            sigma = float(rng.choice([0.05, 0.10, 0.18]))
            cand = normalize_nonneg(base * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma, size=n)))
            cand_conv = fft_convolve(cand)
            sc_c = score_from_conv_norm(cand_conv, idx, n)
            if np.isfinite(sc_c):
                if sc_c < best_score or no_improve > 6000:
                    p = cand
                    conv = cand_conv
                    cur_score = sc_c
                    no_improve = 0

        # Exact validation, only when useful / affordable.
        now = time.monotonic()
        if allow_exact and exact_calls < max_exact_calls and (now - last_exact) >= exact_interval and n <= 1200:
            seq = as_eval_list(p)
            sc = safe_eval(seq)
            exact_calls += 1
            last_exact = now
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_exact:
                best_exact = sc
                best_exact_p = seq

        if step % 20000 == 0:
            print(f"      Coord n={n} step={step} score={cur_score:.6f} best={best_score:.6f} t={time.monotonic()-t0:.1f}s")

    if allow_exact:
        seq = as_eval_list(best_p)
        sc = safe_eval(seq)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_exact:
            best_exact = sc
            best_exact_p = seq

    return best_p, best_score, best_exact, best_exact_p


def calibrate_mode(parent, rng):
    """Infer which coefficient mask best matches evaluate_sequence."""
    tests = []
    n = 32
    tests.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n)
    sp = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    sp[0] = 1.0
    tests.append(sp)
    ramp = np.linspace(0.1, 1.0, n)
    tests.append(normalize_nonneg(ramp))
    comb = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    comb[::2] = 1.0
    tests.append(normalize_nonneg(comb))
    rnd = np.abs(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, size=n)) + 0.1
    tests.append(normalize_nonneg(rnd))

    if parent is not None:
        if len(parent) <= 256:
            tests.append(normalize_nonneg(parent))
        else:
            tests.append(normalize_nonneg(interpolate_sequence(parent, 128)))

    exact_scores = []
    for p in tests:
        exact_scores.append(safe_eval(as_eval_list(p)))

    errors = {}
    for mode in ("full", "off", "positive"):
        err = 0.0
        for p, ev in zip(tests, exact_scores):
            sv = surrogate_score(p, mode)
            if not np.isfinite(ev):
                err += 1000.0
            elif not np.isfinite(sv):
                err += 100.0
            else:
                denom = max(1.0, abs(ev), abs(sv))
                err += abs(ev - sv) / denom
        errors[mode] = err

    if all(e >= 1e9 for e in errors.values()):
        return "full", errors

    best_mode = "full"
    best_err = errors["full"]
    for mode in ("off", "positive"):
        if errors[mode] < best_err:
            best_err = errors[mode]
            best_mode = mode
    return best_mode, errors


def propose_candidate(seed=0, budget_s=2400):
    """Main entry point: search for a high-quality non-negative sequence."""
    t_global = time.monotonic()
    try:
        bud = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        bud = 2400.0
    if not np.isfinite(bud) or bud <= 0.0:
        bud = 2400.0
    deadline = t_global + max(30.0, bud - 25.0)

    if seed is None:
        seed_val = 123456789
    else:
        try:
            seed_val = int(seed)
        except Exception:
            seed_val = 123456789
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed_val)

    parent = get_parent_sequence()
    best_exact = float("inf")
    best_exact_p = None
    best_surr = float("inf")
    best_surr_p = None

    # Parent check.
    if parent is not None:
        seq = as_eval_list(parent)
        sc = safe_eval(seq)
        print(f"Parent: n={len(parent)}, exact={sc:.6f}")
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_exact:
            best_exact = sc
            best_exact_p = seq

    # Infer metric mask.
    mode, errors = calibrate_mode(parent, rng)
    print(f"Inferred mode: {mode} calibration_errors={errors}")

    if parent is not None:
        ps = surrogate_score(parent, mode)
        if ps < best_surr:
            best_surr = ps
            best_surr_p = parent.copy()

    # Simple/important candidates.
    base_n = len(parent) if parent is not None else 512
    if base_n < 32:
        base_n = 32
    simple_n = int(min(max(base_n, 32), 1024))

    cand = np.ones(simple_n, dtype=np.float64)
    cand = normalize_nonneg(cand)
    sc = safe_eval(as_eval_list(cand))
    print(f"Uniform n={simple_n}: exact={sc:.6f}")
    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_exact:
        best_exact = sc
        best_exact_p = as_eval_list(cand)
    ps = surrogate_score(cand, mode)
    if ps < best_surr:
        best_surr = ps
        best_surr_p = cand.copy()

    cand = np.zeros(simple_n, dtype=np.float64)
    cand[0] = 1.0
    cand = normalize_nonneg(cand)
    sc = safe_eval(as_eval_list(cand))
    print(f"Sparse n={simple_n}: exact={sc:.6f}")
    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_exact:
        best_exact = sc
        best_exact_p = as_eval_list(cand)
    ps = surrogate_score(cand, mode)
    if ps < best_surr:
        best_surr = ps
        best_surr_p = cand.copy()

    if (np.isfinite(best_exact) and best_exact <= 1e-9) or (best_surr <= 1e-9):
        print("Found an essentially zero sequence; returning immediately.")
        if best_exact_p is not None:
            return best_exact_p
        return as_eval_list(best_surr_p if best_surr_p is not None else np.ones(simple_n) / simple_n)

    # Choose lengths to explore.
    candidate_lengths = []
    for nn in (base_n, int(base_n * 1.5), int(base_n * 2), int(base_n * 3), 512, 1024, 2048, 4096):
        if nn < 32:
            nn = 32
        if nn > 8192:
            nn = 8192
        candidate_lengths.append(int(nn))
    unique_lengths = sorted(set(candidate_lengths))
    if len(unique_lengths) > 6:
        keep = set()
        keep.add(unique_lengths[0])
        keep.add(unique_lengths[len(unique_lengths) // 2])
        keep.add(unique_lengths[-1])
        if 512 in unique_lengths:
            keep.add(512)
        if 1024 in unique_lengths:
            keep.add(1024)
        unique_lengths = sorted(keep)

    # Build jobs: (surrogate_score, n, uid, p)
    def add_jobs_for_length(n):
        nonlocal_uid = 0
        return None

    # The nested block above is just conceptual; actual code below stays top-level-ish
    # by using local variables only (no nested function definitions).
    job_tuples = []
    uid = 0
    total_phase1_time = max(60.0, (deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.45)
    phase_time = total_phase1_time
    print(f"\n=== Phase 1: log-Adam optimization ===  budget={phase_time:.0f}s")

    # Assign each length a budget based on size.
    length_budget = {}
    weights = []
    for n in unique_lengths:
        w = (n / 512.0) ** 0.75
        length_budget[n] = w
        weights.append(w)
    wsum = float(sum(weights))

    for n in unique_lengths:
        remaining = deadline - time.monotonic() - 60.0
        if remaining <= 30.0:
            break
        allow_here = min(length_budget[n] / wsum * phase_time, remaining)
        if allow_here < 8.0:
            continue
        pool = make_pool(n, parent, rng, mode, max_pool=6 if n <= 768 else 4)
        job_pool = []
        local_uid = 0
        for p in pool:
            sc = surrogate_score(p, mode)
            job_pool.append((sc, uid, p))
            uid += 1
            local_uid += 1
        job_pool.sort()
        # Skip adding duplicates that are too similar? Not necessary.
        job_tuples.extend(job_pool)

    # Select a manageable number of jobs, preserving length diversity.
    selected_jobs = []
    selected_uid = set()
    for n in unique_lengths:
        n_jobs = []
        for item in job_tuples:
            sc, juid, p = item
            if len(p) == n:
                n_jobs.append(item)
        n_jobs.sort()
        take = 2 if n <= 1024 else 1
        for item in n_jobs[:take]:
            juid = item[1]
            if juid not in selected_uid:
                selected_jobs.append(item)
                selected_uid.add(juid)

    # Fill with globally best jobs up to 10-12 total.
    job_tuples.sort()
    for item in job_tuples:
        if len(selected_jobs) >= 12:
            break
        juid = item[1]
        if juid not in selected_uid:
            selected_jobs.append(item)
            selected_uid.add(juid)
    selected_jobs.sort()

    print(f"Selected {len(selected_jobs)} gradient jobs.")

    polish_pool = []
    run_uid = 0
    # Sort selected jobs by expected cost? We already inserted by length. Keep.
    for item in selected_jobs:
        sc, juid, p = item
        n = len(p)
        now = time.monotonic()
        remaining = deadline - now - 90.0
        if remaining <= 10.0:
            break
        # Slightly cap per-job time based on n.
        w = (n / 512.0) ** 0.75
        t_alloc = min(max(10.0, phase_time * w / max(wsum, 1e-9) / max(1.0, float(len(selected_jobs)) * 0.5)), remaining)
        if t_alloc < 4.0:
            continue

        lr = 0.10 if n > 2048 else (0.14 if n > 1024 else 0.18)
        p_best, sc_best = optimize_log_adam(p, mode, t_alloc, rng, tau_factor=8.0, lr_scale=lr)
        p_best = normalize_nonneg(p_best)
        sc_best = surrogate_score(p_best, mode)
        print(f"  Grad job n={len(p)}: score={sc_best:.6f} time_left={deadline - time.monotonic():.0f}s")

        sc_ex = float("inf")
        if len(p_best) <= 1024:
            sc_ex = safe_eval(as_eval_list(p_best))
            if np.isfinite(sc_ex) and sc_ex < best_exact:
                best_exact = sc_ex
                best_exact_p = as_eval_list(p_best)

        if sc_best < best_surr:
            best_surr = sc_best
            best_surr_p = p_best.copy()

        exact_key = sc_ex if np.isfinite(sc_ex) else 1e9
        polish_pool.append((exact_key, sc_best, len(p_best), run_uid, p_best))
        run_uid += 1

        if np.isfinite(best_exact) and best_exact <= 1.0:
            break

    if not polish_pool:
        polish_pool.append((1e9, best_surr, len(parent) if parent is not None else 512, 0,
                            parent if parent is not None else np.ones(512, dtype=np.float64)))

    # Sort polish pool: exact first, then surrogate.
    polish_pool.sort()

    # Phase 2: LP-inspired polish on the best small/medium candidate.
    print(f"\n=== Phase 2: LP-inspired polish ===")
    now = time.monotonic()
    remaining = deadline - now - 120.0
    if remaining > 120.0 and _get_lp_solver() is not None:
        # Pick the best candidate with length <= 1024.
        lp_item = None
        for item in polish_pool:
            exact_key, sc_best, n, uid, p = item
            if n <= 1024:
                lp_item = item
                break
        if lp_item is None:
            lp_item = polish_pool[0]
        exact_key, sc_best, n, uid, p = lp_item
        lp_time = min(160.0, remaining * 0.25)
        p_lp, sc_lp = lp_polish(p, mode, lp_time, rng, max_solves=40, max_n=1024)
        sc_lp = surrogate_score(p_lp, mode)
        print(f"  LP n={len(p_lp)}: score={sc_lp:.6f}")
        if sc_lp < best_surr:
            best_surr = sc_lp
            best_surr_p = p_lp.copy()
        sc_ex = float("inf")
        if len(p_lp) <= 1024:
            sc_ex = safe_eval(as_eval_list(p_lp))
            if np.isfinite(sc_ex) and sc_ex < best_exact:
                best_exact = sc_ex
                best_exact_p = as_eval_list(p_lp)
        exact_key2 = sc_ex if np.isfinite(sc_ex) else 1e9
        polish_pool.append((exact_key2, sc_lp, len(p_lp), run_uid, p_lp))
        run_uid += 1
    else:
        print("  skipped (no sufficiently remaining time or no scipy.linprog)")

    polish_pool.sort()

    # Phase 3: coordinate polish on top candidates.
    print(f"\n=== Phase 3: coordinate polish ===")
    now = time.monotonic()
    coord_budget = max(60.0, deadline - now - 60.0)
    # Unique representatives: prefer low exact then surrogate, but avoid overdoing same length a bit.
    selected_polish = []
    seen_uid = set()
    for item in polish_pool:
        if item[3] in seen_uid:
            continue
        selected_polish.append(item)
        seen_uid.add(item[3])
        if len(selected_polish) == 3:
            break

    fracs = [0.55, 0.30, 0.15]
    total_adv = 0.0
    for k in range(len(fracs)):
        total_adv += fracs[k]
    if total_adv <= 0.0:
        fracs = [1.0]

    for k, item in enumerate(selected_polish):
        now = time.monotonic()
        remaining = deadline - now - 45.0
        if remaining <= 20.0:
            break
        exact_key, sc_best, n, uid, p = item
        t_alloc = max(20.0, remaining * fracs[k])
        allow_exact = (mode != "full") and (n <= 1200)
        allow_exact = allow_exact and (remaining > 60.0)
        p_c, sc_c, ex_c, seq_c = coordinate_polish(
            p, mode, t_alloc, rng,
            allow_exact=allow_exact,
            exact_interval=2.0,
            max_exact_calls=80
        )
        sc_c = surrogate_score(p_c, mode)
        print(f"  Coord candidate {k}: n={len(p_c)} score={sc_c:.6f} exact={ex_c if np.isfinite(ex_c) else 'inf'}")
        if sc_c < best_surr:
            best_surr = sc_c
            best_surr_p = p_c.copy()
        if np.isfinite(ex_c) and ex_c < best_exact:
            best_exact = ex_c
            best_exact_p = as_eval_list(p_c)
        policy_key = ex_c if np.isfinite(ex_c) else 1e9
        polish_pool.append((policy_key, sc_c, len(p_c), run_uid, p_c))
        run_uid += 1

    # Phase 4: final extra polish on the current best if time remains.
    print(f"\n=== Phase 4: final extra polish ===")
    now = time.monotonic()
    remaining = deadline - now - 25.0
    if remaining > 45.0:
        if best_surr_p is not None:
            base_p = best_surr_p
        elif best_exact_p is not None:
            base_p = np.asarray(best_exact_p, dtype=np.float64)
        elif parent is not None:
            base_p = parent
        else:
            base_p = np.ones(512, dtype=np.float64)
        base_p = normalize_nonneg(base_p)
        p_e, sc_e, ex_e, seq_e = coordinate_polish(
            base_p, mode, min(120.0, remaining - 20.0), rng,
            allow_exact=(mode != "full" and len(base_p) <= 1200)
        )
        sc_e = surrogate_score(p_e, mode)
        print(f"  Extra polish: n={len(p_e)} score={sc_e:.6f} exact={ex_e if np.isfinite(ex_e) else 'inf'}")
        if sc_e < best_surr:
            best_surr = sc_e
            best_surr_p = p_e.copy()
        if np.isfinite(ex_e) and ex_e < best_exact:
            best_exact = ex_e
            best_exact_p = as_eval_list(p_e)

    # Final validation / selection among all known good candidates.
    final_candidates = []
    if best_exact_p is not None:
        final_candidates.append(np.asarray(best_exact_p, dtype=np.float64))
    if best_surr_p is not None:
        final_candidates.append(best_surr_p)
    if parent is not None:
        final_candidates.append(parent)
    final_candidates.append(np.ones(simple_n, dtype=np.float64) / simple_n)

    for arr in final_candidates:
        if arr is None:
            continue
        seq = as_eval_list(arr)
        sc = safe_eval(seq)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_exact:
            best_exact = sc
            best_exact_p = seq
            print(f"Final validation improved: {sc:.6f} n={len(seq)}")

    if best_exact_p is not None and len(best_exact_p) > 0 and np.isfinite(best_exact):
        return best_exact_p

    # Fallback if evaluate_sequence is unavailable or invalid.
    if best_surr_p is not None:
        out = as_eval_list(best_surr_p)
        return out if out else [1.0] * 512
    if parent is not None:
        return as_eval_list(parent)
    return [1.0] * 512
```
